            "opt_prim_ms", "opt_alt_ms", "osqp_iters", "servo_pulse_us", "f_tof_min",
            "anomaly_flags", "shield_active", "is_on_ramp", "is_wrong_way",
            "mcu_main_connected", "mcu_tof_connected", "stm32_front", "stm32_rear",
            "imu_online", "override_active",
        ]

        if "csv" in req_formats:
            csv_path = dest / "frames.csv"
            with open(csv_path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                writer = csv.writer(fh)
                tof_cols = [f"tof_raw_{i}" for i in range(16)]
                writer.writerow(scalar_cols + tof_cols)
                for f in self.frames:
                    row = [
                        int(f[c]) if isinstance(f[c], bool) else f[c]
                        for c in scalar_cols
                    ] + [round(x, 3) for x in f["tof_ranges_raw"]]
                    writer.writerow(row)
            outputs["csv"] = str(csv_path)

        if "npz" in req_formats:
            n = self.n_frames
            best_arr = np.full((n, 31, 2), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
            cl_arr = np.full((n, 31, 2), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
            cr_arr = np.full((n, 31, 2), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)
            pose_arr = np.full((n, 3), np.nan, dtype=np.float32)

            for i, f in enumerate(self.frames):
                for src, dst in (
                    (f["best_line"], best_arr),
                    (f["corridor_left"], cl_arr),
                    (f["corridor_right"], cr_arr),
                ):
                    for j, pt in enumerate(src[:31]):
                        if isinstance(pt, (list, tuple)) and len(pt) >= 2:
                            dst[i, j, 0] = _safe_float(pt[0], math.nan)
                            dst[i, j, 1] = _safe_float(pt[1], math.nan)
                if isinstance(f["pose"], (list, tuple)) and len(f["pose"]) >= 3:
                    pose_arr[i, 0] = _safe_float(f["pose"][0], math.nan)
                    pose_arr[i, 1] = _safe_float(f["pose"][1], math.nan)
                    pose_arr[i, 2] = _safe_float(f["pose"][2], math.nan)

            npz_path = dest / "arrays.npz"
            np.savez_compressed(
                npz_path,
                frame_id=self.frame_ids,
                timestamp=self.timestamps,
                sim_time=self.sim_times,
                driving_time=np.array([f["driving_time"] for f in self.frames], dtype=np.float64),
                is_driving=self.is_driving,
                mode=np.array([f["mode"] for f in self.frames]),
                status=np.array([f["status"] for f in self.frames]),
                v_cmd=self.v_cmd,
                v_tgt=self.v_tgt,
                v_real=self.v_real,
                steer_cmd_deg=self.steer_cmd,
                steer_actual_deg=self.steer_act,
                rpms_cmd=self.rpms_cmd,
                rpms_act=self.rpms_act,
                yaw_deg=np.array([f["yaw_deg"] for f in self.frames], dtype=np.float64),
                pitch_deg=self.pitch_deg,
                roll_deg=self.roll_deg,
                gyro_z=self.gyro_z,
                accel_fwd=self.accel_fwd,
                accel_lat=self.accel_lat,
                slip_ratio=self.slip_ratio,
                plan_ms=self.plan_ms,
                step_ms=self.step_ms,
                map_ms=self.map_ms,
                scout_ms=self.scout_ms,
                f_tof_min=self.f_tof_min,
                anomaly_flags=np.array([f["anomaly_flags"] for f in self.frames], dtype=np.int32),
                shield_active=self.shield_active,
                is_on_ramp=self.is_on_ramp,
                is_wrong_way=self.is_wrong_way,
                tof_ranges=self.tof_ranges,
                tof_ranges_raw=self.tof_ranges_raw,
                best_line=best_arr,
                corridor_left=cl_arr,
                corridor_right=cr_arr,
                pose=pose_arr,
            )
            outputs["npz"] = str(npz_path)

        if "parquet" in req_formats:
            try:
                import pyarrow  # noqa: F401
                import pandas as pd

                records = []
                for f in self.frames:
                    d = {c: (int(f[c]) if isinstance(f[c], bool) else f[c]) for c in scalar_cols}
                    for i in range(16):
                        d[f"tof_raw_{i}"] = round(f["tof_ranges_raw"][i], 3)
                    records.append(d)
                pq_path = dest / "frames.parquet"
                pd.DataFrame.from_records(records).to_parquet(pq_path, index=False, engine="pyarrow")
                outputs["parquet"] = str(pq_path)
            except ImportError:
                outputs["parquet"] = "skipped (pyarrow not installed)"
            except Exception as exc:
                outputs["parquet"] = f"skipped ({exc})"

        return outputs


def render_ascii_radar(
    frame: Dict[str, Any],
    width: int = 49,
    height: int = 23,
) -> str:
    """
    Renders a 2D car-frame top-down ASCII radar (+Y up/forward, +X right).
    Bounds: x in [-1.25, +1.25] m, y in [0.0, 2.20] m.
    Legend: A=car (0,0), #=ToF hit, <=corridor_left, >=corridor_right, *=best_line, G=gap/target, !=shield.
    """
    x_min, x_max = -1.25, 1.25
    y_min, y_max = 0.0, 2.20
    grid = [[" " for _ in range(width)] for _ in range(height)]

    def _plot(x: float, y: float, ch: str, overwrite: bool = True):
        if not (math.isfinite(x) and math.isfinite(y)):
            return
        if x < x_min or x > x_max or y < y_min or y > y_max:
            return
        col = int(round((x - x_min) / (x_max - x_min) * (width - 1)))
        row = int(round((y_max - y) / (y_max - y_min) * (height - 1)))
        col = max(0, min(width - 1, col))
        row = max(0, min(height - 1, row))
        if overwrite or grid[row][col] == " ":
            grid[row][col] = ch

    # Subtle centerline guide
    mid_col = int(round((0.0 - x_min) / (x_max - x_min) * (width - 1)))
    for r in range(height):
        grid[r][mid_col] = ":"

    # Corridor boundaries
    for pt in frame.get("corridor_left") or []:
        if isinstance(pt, (list, tuple)) and len(pt) >= 2:
            _plot(_safe_float(pt[0]), _safe_float(pt[1]), "<")
    for pt in frame.get("corridor_right") or []:
        if isinstance(pt, (list, tuple)) and len(pt) >= 2:
            _plot(_safe_float(pt[0]), _safe_float(pt[1]), ">")

    # Best line trajectory
    for pt in frame.get("best_line") or []:
        if isinstance(pt, (list, tuple)) and len(pt) >= 2:
            _plot(_safe_float(pt[0]), _safe_float(pt[1]), "*")

    # Active shield trajectory if engaged
    payload = frame.get("payload") if isinstance(frame.get("payload"), dict) else {}
    shield = payload.get("shield") if isinstance(payload.get("shield"), dict) else {}
    if shield.get("active"):
        chosen_name = shield.get("chosen_name")
        if chosen_name and isinstance(shield.get(chosen_name), list):
            for pt in shield[chosen_name]:
                if isinstance(pt, (list, tuple)) and len(pt) >= 2:
                    _plot(_safe_float(pt[0]), _safe_float(pt[1]), "!")

    # Gap / primary target
    targets = frame.get("targets") or []
    if targets and isinstance(targets[0], (list, tuple)) and len(targets[0]) >= 2:
        _plot(_safe_float(targets[0][0]), _safe_float(targets[0][1]), "G")
    elif isinstance(frame.get("gap"), (list, tuple)) and len(frame["gap"]) >= 2:
        _plot(_safe_float(frame["gap"][0]), _safe_float(frame["gap"][1]), "G")

    # 16 cross-eyed ToF obstacle hits
    tof_vals = frame.get("tof_ranges") or frame.get("tof_ranges_raw") or []
    for i, (_, mx, my, hdg) in enumerate(FRONT_TOF_SENSOR_RIG):
        if i >= len(tof_vals):
            break
        d = _safe_float(tof_vals[i], 0.0)
        if 0.01 < d < 1.78:
            hx = mx + d * math.cos(hdg)
            hy = my + d * math.sin(hdg)
            _plot(hx, hy, "#")

    # Vehicle origin
    _plot(0.0, 0.0, "A")

    lines = [
        f"Top-Down Radar (Frame #{frame['frame_id']} | mode={frame['mode']} | x=[-1.25..+1.25]m, y=[0..2.2]m)",
        "+" + "-" * width + "+",
    ]
    for r, row_chars in enumerate(grid):
        y_val = y_max - r / max(1, height - 1) * (y_max - y_min)
        suffix = f" {y_val:.1f}m" if r in (0, height // 2, height - 1) else ""
        lines.append("|" + "".join(row_chars) + "|" + suffix)
    lines.append("+" + "-" * width + "+")
    lines.append("Legend: A=robot  #=ToF hit  <=left_wall  >=right_wall  *=best_line  G=target/gap  !=shield")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def compare_sessions(db_a: str | Path, db_b: str | Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Computes side-by-side KPI deltas and config/git diff between session A and session B."""
    sa = FlightSession(db_a)
    sb = FlightSession(db_b)
    da = sa.build_digest()
    db = sb.build_digest()

    ka, kb = da["kpis"], db["kpis"]
    ca, cb = ka["continuity"], kb["continuity"]

    def _rate_per_min(ep_list: List[Dict[str, Any]], cat: str, drv_s: float) -> float:
        cnt = sum(1 for e in ep_list if e["category"] == cat)
        return round(cnt / max(1e-3, drv_s / 60.0), 2)

    eps_a = sa.extract_episodes()
    eps_b = sb.extract_episodes()

    metrics = [
        ("Driving Seconds (s)", ca["driving_seconds"], cb["driving_seconds"]),
        ("Distance (m)", ca["distance_m"], cb["distance_m"]),
        ("Mean Speed v_real (m/s)", ka.get("v_real", {}).get("mean", 0.0), kb.get("v_real", {}).get("mean", 0.0)),
        ("Max Speed v_real (m/s)", ka.get("v_real", {}).get("max", 0.0), kb.get("v_real", {}).get("max", 0.0)),
        ("Speed RMSE (m/s)", ka.get("v_err_rmse", 0.0), kb.get("v_err_rmse", 0.0)),
        ("Steer RMSE (deg)", ka.get("steer_err_rmse", 0.0), kb.get("steer_err_rmse", 0.0)),
        ("Plan Time p95 (ms)", ka.get("timing_ms", {}).get("plan_ms", {}).get("p95", 0.0), kb.get("timing_ms", {}).get("plan_ms", {}).get("p95", 0.0)),
        ("Shield Active (%)", ka.get("shield_pct", 0.0), kb.get("shield_pct", 0.0)),
        ("On-Ramp (%)", ka.get("imu", {}).get("is_on_ramp_pct", 0.0), kb.get("imu", {}).get("is_on_ramp_pct", 0.0)),
        ("Emergency Eps / min", _rate_per_min(eps_a, "EMERGENCY_RECOVERY", ca["driving_seconds"]), _rate_per_min(eps_b, "EMERGENCY_RECOVERY", cb["driving_seconds"])),
        ("Shield Eps / min", _rate_per_min(eps_a, "SHIELD_EVADE", ca["driving_seconds"]), _rate_per_min(eps_b, "SHIELD_EVADE", cb["driving_seconds"])),
        ("Stall Eps / min", _rate_per_min(eps_a, "STALL", ca["driving_seconds"]), _rate_per_min(eps_b, "STALL", cb["driving_seconds"])),
    ]

    comparisons = []
    for name, va, vb in metrics:
        delta = round(float(vb) - float(va), 3)
        pct = round(delta / abs(float(va)) * 100.0, 1) if abs(float(va)) > 1e-6 else None
        comparisons.append({"metric": name, "a": va, "b": vb, "delta": delta, "pct_change": pct})

    all_modes = sorted(set(ka.get("modes", {})) | set(kb.get("modes", {})))
    mode_shares = []
    na = max(1, sa.n_frames)
    nb = max(1, sb.n_frames)
    for m in all_modes:
        pa = round(ka.get("modes", {}).get(m, 0) / na * 100.0, 1)
        pb = round(kb.get("modes", {}).get(m, 0) / nb * 100.0, 1)
        mode_shares.append({"mode": m, "a_pct": pa, "b_pct": pb, "delta_pct": round(pb - pa, 1)})

    return {
        "session_a": da["session"],
        "session_b": db["session"],
        "metrics": comparisons,
        "mode_shares": mode_shares,
        "git_diff": {
            "a": da["session"].get("git_commit", ""),
            "b": db["session"].get("git_commit", ""),
        },
        "config_diff": _diff_dicts(sa.config, sb.config),
    }


def _diff_dicts(a: Dict[str, Any], b: Dict[str, Any], prefix: str = "") -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    diffs: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for k in sorted(set(a) | set(b)):
        key_path = f"{prefix}.{k}" if prefix else str(k)
        va, vb = a.get(k), b.get(k)
        if isinstance(va, dict) and isinstance(vb, dict):
            diffs.extend(_diff_dicts(va, vb, key_path))
        elif va != vb:
            diffs.append({"key": key_path, "a": va, "b": vb})
    return diffs


# ==============================================================================
# COMPACT MARKDOWN / TABLE FORMATTERS FOR AI & TERMINAL INGESTION
# ==============================================================================

def format_digest_markdown(digest: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    s = digest["session"]
    k = digest["kpis"]
    diags = digest["diagnostics"]
    eps = digest["episodes"]

    lines: List[str] = []
    lines.append(f"# Flight Debug Digest: {s['filename']}")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("## 1. Session Overview")
    lines.append(
        f"- **Session ID**: `{s['session_id']}` | **Schema**: v{s['schema_version']} | "
        f"**Git**: `{s['git_commit'] or 'unknown'}` | **Size**: {s['file_size_mb']} MB"
    )
    lines.append(
        f"- **Frames**: {s['total_frames']} total (`{s['frame_range'][0]}..{s['frame_range'][1]}`), "
        f"{s['driving_frames']} driving | **Rate**: {s['effective_hz']} Hz"
    )
    lines.append(
        f"- **Duration & Distance**: {s['wall_duration_s']}s wall, {s['driving_seconds']}s driving, "
        f"est. **{s['distance_m']} m**"
    )
    lines.append(
        f"- **Authority / Links**: override_active={s['override_frames']} frames | "
        f"active_link_drops={s['link_drop_frames']} frames | incidents={digest['episodes_total']}"
    )
    lines.append("")

    lines.append("## 2. Automated Diagnostics")
    if not diags:
        lines.append("- `[INFO]` No significant faults detected.")
    else:
        for d in diags:
            samp = f" (frames: {d['sample_frames']})" if d["sample_frames"] else ""
            lines.append(f"- **[{d['severity']}] `{d['code']}`**: {d['title']}{samp}")
            lines.append(f"  - {d['evidence']}")
    lines.append("")

    lines.append("## 3. KPI Summary")
    lines.append("| Subsystem | Metric | Value |")
    lines.append("| :--- | :--- | :--- |")
    if k.get("v_real"):
        vr = k["v_real"]
        vc = k["v_cmd"]
        lines.append(f"| Speed | v_real (min/mean/p95/max) | {vr['min']} / {vr['mean']} / {vr['p95']} / {vr['max']} m/s (cmd mean={vc['mean']}, RMSE={k['v_err_rmse']}) |")
        lines.append(f"| Steering | tracking RMSE | {k['steer_err_rmse']} deg |")
        rm = k["rpm_act_abs_mean"]
        re = k["rpm_tracking_mae"]
        lines.append(f"| Wheels | mean |RPM_act| | FL={rm['FL']}, FR={rm['FR']}, RL={rm['RL']}, RR={rm['RR']} (MAE: {re['FL']}/{re['FR']}/{re['RL']}/{re['RR']}) |")
        imu = k["imu"]
        lines.append(f"| IMU / Ramp | pitch / |gyro_z| / |a_lat| | pitch={imu['pitch_deg']['mean']} [{imu['pitch_deg']['min']}..{imu['pitch_deg']['max']}] deg, gyro_z_p95={imu['gyro_z']['p95']}, a_lat_p95={imu['accel_lat']['p95']}, on_ramp={imu['is_on_ramp_pct']}% |")
        tm = k["timing_ms"]
        lines.append(f"| Timing (ms) | plan / step / map / scout | plan={tm['plan_ms']['mean']}/{tm['plan_ms']['p95']}/{tm['plan_ms']['max']}, step={tm['step_ms']['mean']}/{tm['step_ms']['p95']}/{tm['step_ms']['max']}, map={tm['map_ms']['mean']}, scout={tm['scout_ms']['mean']} |")
        lines.append(f"| Safety | f_tof_min / shield / wrong_way | tof_min={k['f_tof_min']['min']} (p95={k['f_tof_min']['p95']}), shield={k['shield_pct']}%, wrong_way={k['wrong_way_frames']}f |")
    top_modes = ", ".join(f"{m}:{cnt}" for m, cnt in list(k.get("modes", {}).items())[:8])
    top_status = ", ".join(f"{st}:{cnt}" for st, cnt in list(k.get("statuses", {}).items())[:6])
    lines.append(f"| Modes | distribution | {top_modes} |")
    lines.append(f"| Status | planner status | {top_status} |")
    lines.append("")

    lines.append(f"## 4. Top Incident Episodes ({len(eps)} shown of {digest['episodes_total']})")
    if not eps:
        lines.append("- None detected.")
    else:
        lines.append("| ID | Severity | Category | Frames | Sim t (s) | Dur | min_tof | v_real | Modes | Lead-in -> Outcome |")
        lines.append("| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |")
        for e in eps:
            pm = e["peak_metrics"]
            m_str = "->".join(e["modes_visited"][:3]) + ("..." if len(e["modes_visited"]) > 3 else "")
            li = e["lead_in"]
            oc = e["outcome"]
            lo_str = f"{li['mode']}({li['v_real']}m/s) -> {oc['mode']}({oc['v_real']}m/s)"
            lines.append(
                f"| {e['episode_id']} | {e['severity']} | {e['category']} | {e['start_frame']}..{e['end_frame']} ({e['frame_count']}f) | "
                f"{e['sim_start']}..{e['sim_end']} | {e['duration_s']}s | {pm['min_f_tof']}m | {pm['min_v_real']}..{pm['max_v_real']} | {m_str} | {lo_str} |"
            )
    lines.append("")

    lines.append("## 5. ToF 16-Channel Health")
    chs = k.get("tof_channels") or []
    if chs:
        left_meds = [f"{c['ch']}:{c['median']:.2f}" for c in chs[:8]]
        right_meds = [f"{c['ch']}:{c['median']:.2f}" for c in chs[8:]]
        lines.append(f"- **Left Pod (0..7) medians (m)**: {', '.join(left_meds)}")
        lines.append(f"- **Right Pod (8..15) medians (m)**: {', '.join(right_meds)}")
        bad = [c for c in chs if c["zero_pct"] > 5.0]
        if bad:
            lines.append("- **Non-trivial zero readings (>5%)**: " + ", ".join(f"ch{c['ch']}={c['zero_pct']}%" for c in bad))
    lines.append("")

    lines.append("## 6. Deduplicated MCU / System Logs")
    logs = digest.get("mcu_logs") or []
    non_servo = [m for m in logs if not m["message"].startswith('{"pin":')]
    shown = non_servo[:20] if non_servo else logs[:20]
    if not shown:
        lines.append("- None recorded.")
    else:
        for m in shown:
            lines.append(f"- `f#{m['frame_id']} (t={m['sim_time']}s)` [{m['severity']}] {m['message']}")
        if len(logs) > len(shown):
            lines.append(f"- _({len(logs) - len(shown)} additional log entries omitted; see --json or export)_")
    lines.append("")

    if digest.get("labels"):
        lines.append("## 7. Stored Labels")
        for lbl in digest["labels"]:
            lines.append(f"- `#{lbl['start_frame']}..#{lbl['end_frame']}` **{lbl['tag']}** ({lbl['author']}): {lbl['note']}")
        lines.append("")

    if eps:
        lines.append("## 8. Next Drill-Down Commands")
        lead_frames = max(15, int(round(1.5 * (s["effective_hz"] or 60.0))))
        for e in eps[:2]:
            w0 = max(1, e["start_frame"] - lead_frames)
            w1 = e["end_frame"] + 15
            lines.append(f"- `python tools/ai_debug_suite.py window \"{s['db_path']}\" {w0} {w1}`  # Inspect {e['episode_id']} ({e['category']} + ~1.5s lead-in)")
            lines.append(f"- `python tools/ai_debug_suite.py frame \"{s['db_path']}\" {e['start_frame']}`  # Radar at episode start")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def format_episodes_markdown(episodes: List[Dict[str, Any]], db_name: str) -> str:
    lines = [f"# Incident Episodes: {db_name} ({len(episodes)} total)", ""]
    if not episodes:
        lines.append("No incident episodes matched filters.")
        return "\n".join(lines)
    for e in episodes:
        pm = e["peak_metrics"]
        li = e["lead_in"]
        oc = e["outcome"]
        lines.append(
            f"### {e['episode_id']} [{e['severity']}] {e['category']} — frames {e['start_frame']}..{e['end_frame']} "
            f"({e['frame_count']}f, {e['duration_s']}s, sim_t={e['sim_start']}..{e['sim_end']}s)"
        )
        lines.append(
            f"- **Modes**: {' -> '.join(e['modes_visited'])} | **Peaks**: min_tof={pm['min_f_tof']}m, "
            f"v_real=[{pm['min_v_real']}..{pm['max_v_real']}] m/s, max|steer|={pm['max_abs_steer']} deg, "
            f"max|slip|={pm['max_abs_slip']}, max_plan={pm['max_plan_ms']} ms, max_step={pm['max_step_ms']} ms"
        )
        lines.append(
            f"- **Lead-in (#{li['frame_id']})**: {li['mode']} v={li['v_real']}m/s str={li['steer_deg']}deg tof={li['f_tof_min']}m -> "
            f"**Outcome (#{oc['frame_id']})**: {oc['mode']} v={oc['v_real']}m/s str={oc['steer_deg']}deg tof={oc['f_tof_min']}m"
        )
        if e["mcu_logs"]:
            lines.append("- **Logs**: " + " | ".join(f"f#{m['frame_id']}: {m['message']}" for m in e["mcu_logs"][:3]))
        lines.append("")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def format_window_table(rows: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    if not rows:
        return "(no frames in range)"
    header = "  fid |  sim_t | mode                    | v_cmd / real | str c /  a | f_tof |  slip | plan_ms | shield        | gap     | flags"
    sep = "-" * len(header)
    out = [header, sep]
    for r in rows:
        fl = ",".join(r["reasons"]) if r["reasons"] else "-"
        out.append(
            f"{r['frame_id']:5d} | {r['sim_time']:6.2f} | {r['mode']:<23} | "
            f"{r['v_cmd']:5.2f} /{r['v_real']:5.2f} | {r['steer_cmd_deg']:5.1f} /{r['steer_actual_deg']:5.1f} | "
            f"{r['f_tof_min']:5.2f} | {r['slip_ratio']:5.2f} | {r['plan_ms']:7.2f} | "
            f"{r['shield']:<13} | {r['gap']:<7} | {fl}"
        )
    return "\n".join(out)


def format_frame_markdown(info: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    m = info["motion"]
    w = info["wheels"]
    imu = info["imu"]
    tm = info["timing_ms"]
    sh = info["shield"]
    pl = info["planner"]
    lk = info["links"]

    lines = [
        f"# Frame #{info['frame_id']} (sim_t={info['sim_time']:.3f}s | mode={info['mode']} | status={info['status'] or 'NONE'})",
        "",
        f"- **Motion**: v_cmd={m['v_cmd']} | v_tgt={m['v_tgt']} | v_real={m['v_real']} m/s | steer_cmd={m['steer_cmd_deg']} | steer_act={m['steer_actual_deg']} deg | servo={m['servo_pulse_us']} us | pose={m['pose']}",
        f"- **Wheels (cmd -> act, err)**: FL={w['FL']['cmd']}->{w['FL']['act']} ({w['FL']['err']:+.0f}) | FR={w['FR']['cmd']}->{w['FR']['act']} ({w['FR']['err']:+.0f}) | RL={w['RL']['cmd']}->{w['RL']['act']} ({w['RL']['err']:+.0f}) | RR={w['RR']['cmd']}->{w['RR']['act']} ({w['RR']['err']:+.0f})",
        f"- **IMU / Slip**: yaw={imu['yaw_deg']} pitch={imu['pitch_deg']} roll={imu['roll_deg']} deg | gyro_z={imu['gyro_z']} | a_fwd={imu['accel_fwd']} a_lat={imu['accel_lat']} | slip={imu['slip_state']} ({imu['slip_ratio']})",
        f"- **Timing (ms)**: plan={tm['plan_ms']} step={tm['step_ms']} map={tm['map_ms']} scout={tm['scout_ms']} opt_p={tm['opt_prim_ms']} opt_a={tm['opt_alt_ms']} iters={tm['osqp_iters']}",
        f"- **Shield**: active={sh['active']} chosen={sh['chosen_name'] or '-'} rev={sh['reverse_suggested']} | branches={sh['branches_safe']}",
        f"- **Links / Flags**: main={lk['mcu_main_connected']} tof={lk['mcu_tof_connected']} stmF={lk['stm32_front']} stmR={lk['stm32_rear']} imu={lk['imu_online']} | ramp={lk['is_on_ramp']} wrong_way={lk['is_wrong_way']} override={lk['override_active']}",
        f"- **Anomalies**: {', '.join(info['anomaly_reasons']) if info['anomaly_reasons'] else 'NONE'} | f_tof_min={info['f_tof_min']}m",
        f"- **Gap / Targets**: gap={pl['gap']} | targets={pl['targets']}",
    ]
    if pl["corridor_samples"]:
        cs = ", ".join(f"{c['zone']}(w={c['width_m']}m)" for c in pl["corridor_samples"])
        lines.append(f"- **Corridor**: {cs} | best_line_pts={pl['best_line_len']}")
    if info["mcu_msg"] or info["mcu_log"]:
        lines.append(f"- **MCU msg**: `{info['mcu_msg']}` | recent_log={info['mcu_log']}")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("Left Pod (ch 0..7):  " + ", ".join(f"c{c['ch']}={c['raw_m']:.2f}" for c in info["tof_channels"][:8]))
    lines.append("Right Pod (ch 8..15): " + ", ".join(f"c{c['ch']}={c['raw_m']:.2f}" for c in info["tof_channels"][8:]))
    if "ascii_radar" in info:
        lines.append("")
        lines.append(info["ascii_radar"])
    return "\n".join(lines)


def format_comparison_markdown(comp: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    sa = comp["session_a"]
    sb = comp["session_b"]
    lines = [
        f"# Session A/B Comparison",
        f"- **A**: `{sa['filename']}` (frames={sa['total_frames']}, git=`{sa['git_commit'] or 'unknown'}`)",
        f"- **B**: `{sb['filename']}` (frames={sb['total_frames']}, git=`{sb['git_commit'] or 'unknown'}`)",
        "",
        "| Metric | Session A | Session B | Delta (B - A) | % Change |",
        "| :--- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |",
    ]
    for m in comp["metrics"]:
        pct_s = f"{m['pct_change']:+.1f}%" if m["pct_change"] is not None else "-"
        lines.append(f"| {m['metric']} | {m['a']} | {m['b']} | {m['delta']:+.3f} | {pct_s} |")
    lines.append("")
    lines.append("## Mode Share Changes")
    lines.append("| Mode | A % | B % | Delta |")
    lines.append("| :--- | ---: | ---: | ---: |")
    for ms in comp["mode_shares"]:
        lines.append(f"| {ms['mode']} | {ms['a_pct']}% | {ms['b_pct']}% | {ms['delta_pct']:+.1f}% |")
    if comp["config_diff"]:
        lines.append("")
        lines.append("## Config Differences")
        for d in comp["config_diff"][:20]:
            lines.append(f"- `{d['key']}`: `{d['a']}` -> `{d['b']}`")
    return "\n".join(lines)


=== FILE: tools/measure_browser_latency.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
================================================================================
FOLKRACE BROWSER LATENCY & TELEMETRY STREAM PROFILER
================================================================================
Measures client-side latency, arrival jitter, packet delivery consistency,
and rendering pipeline timing exactly as experienced by a browser (e.g. Firefox).

Features:
  1. WebSocket Streaming Benchmark (ws://robot:8080/ws_live):
     - Sub-millisecond RFC 6455 packet arrival intervals.
     - Frame arrival jitter and deviation from nominal 20 Hz (50.0 ms) target.
     - Stutter detection (intervals > 66.7 ms) and hitches (> 100.0 ms).
     - End-to-end client latency (Flight-to-Glass: Robot TS -> Client Arrival -> Parse).
     - Full client pipeline simulation: Header unpack + Zlib decompression + Canvas LUT.
     - High-precision percentiles: Min, P50, P90, P95, P99, P99.9, and Max.
  2. HTTP Polling Benchmark (http://robot:8080/api/live_bin & /api/live):
     - Measures Request-Response RTT distribution across polling frequencies.
     - Quantifies the jitter and TCP connection pooling penalties of HTTP fallback.
  3. Real In-Browser Firefox Harness (--firefox):
     - Automatically launches Firefox, connects to the Tactical Mission Control GUI,
       and collects live performance.now() metrics directly from the browser DOM.

Usage:
  python tools/measure_browser_latency.py                      # Full side-by-side benchmark (10s)
  python tools/measure_browser_latency.py --mode ws            # WebSocket streaming benchmark only
  python tools/measure_browser_latency.py --mode http          # HTTP polling benchmark only
  python tools/measure_browser_latency.py --firefox            # Run benchmark inside real Firefox
  python tools/measure_browser_latency.py --duration 15        # Benchmark over 15 seconds
  python tools/measure_browser_latency.py --host 192.168.20.120 # Specify target Pi host
================================================================================
"""

import os
import sys
import time
import json
import socket
import struct
import zlib
import base64
import argparse
import statistics
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Any

# Ensure UTF-8 output on Windows terminals
if sys.platform == "win32" and hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
    try:
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
    except Exception:
        pass

ROOT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
DEFAULT_CANDIDATES = ["192.168.20.120", "192.168.20.117", "robot", "robot.local", "127.0.0.1"]
DEFAULT_PORT = 8080

FIREFOX_PATHS = [
    r"C:\Program Files\Mozilla Firefox\firefox.exe",
    r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Mozilla Firefox\firefox.exe",
    os.path.expandvars(r"%LOCALAPPDATA%\Mozilla Firefox\firefox.exe"),
]


def resolve_host(preferred: Optional[str] = None, port: int = DEFAULT_PORT) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    """Auto-detects active Raspberry Pi debug server host and base URL."""
    candidates = [preferred] if preferred else DEFAULT_CANDIDATES
    for h in candidates:
        if not h:
            continue
        try:
            url = f"http://{h}:{port}/api/status"
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "FolkraceLatencyTester/1.0"})
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=1.5) as resp:
                if resp.status == 200:
                    return h, f"http://{h}:{port}"
        except Exception:
            continue
    # Fallback to preferred or first candidate
    fallback = preferred or DEFAULT_CANDIDATES[0]
    return fallback, f"http://{fallback}:{port}"


def compute_percentiles(arr: List[float]) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """Computes standard latency percentiles for an array of float measurements."""
    if not arr:
        return {"count": 0, "min": 0.0, "mean": 0.0, "p50": 0.0, "p90": 0.0, "p95": 0.0, "p99": 0.0, "p999": 0.0, "max": 0.0}
    s = sorted(arr)
    n = len(s)
    return {
        "count": n,
        "min": s[0],
        "mean": statistics.mean(s),
        "p50": s[int(n * 0.50)],
        "p90": s[min(n - 1, int(n * 0.90))],
        "p95": s[min(n - 1, int(n * 0.95))],
        "p99": s[min(n - 1, int(n * 0.99))],
        "p999": s[min(n - 1, int(n * 0.999))],
        "max": s[-1],
    }


def benchmark_websocket_stream(
    host: str,
    port: int = DEFAULT_PORT,
    duration_s: float = 10.0,
    target_hz: float = 20.0,
    verbose: bool = True,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Connects to the Pi 4 WebSocket endpoint (ws://host:port/ws_live) and records
    inter-frame arrival intervals, jitter, packet sizes, client parsing, and
    simulated canvas rendering durations.
    """
    if verbose:
        print(f"\n[WebSocket Profiler] Connecting to ws://{host}:{port}/ws_live...")

    sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_TCP, socket.TCP_NODELAY, 1)
    sock.settimeout(3.0)

    t_connect0 = time.perf_counter()
    try:
        sock.connect((host, port))
    except Exception as e:
        if verbose:
            print(f"[WebSocket Error] Failed to connect to {host}:{port}: {e}")
        return {"error": str(e), "connected": False}

    # RFC 6455 Client Handshake
    sec_key = base64.b64encode(os.urandom(16)).decode()
    handshake = (
        f"GET /ws_live HTTP/1.1\r\n"
        f"Host: {host}:{port}\r\n"
        f"Upgrade: websocket\r\n"
        f"Connection: Upgrade\r\n"
        f"Sec-WebSocket-Key: {sec_key}\r\n"
        f"Sec-WebSocket-Version: 13\r\n\r\n"
    )
    sock.sendall(handshake.encode("utf-8"))

    # Read HTTP Response
    buf = b""
    while b"\r\n\r\n" not in buf:
        chunk = sock.recv(1024)
        if not chunk:
            sock.close()
            return {"error": "Server closed during handshake", "connected": False}
        buf += chunk

    hdr_data, buf = buf.split(b"\r\n\r\n", 1)
    dt_handshake = (time.perf_counter() - t_connect0) * 1000.0
    status_line = hdr_data.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore").splitlines()[0]
    if "101" not in status_line:
        sock.close()
        return {"error": f"Bad handshake response: {status_line}", "connected": False}

    if verbose:
        print(f"[WebSocket Profiler] Connected in {dt_handshake:.1f} ms. Streaming for {duration_s:.1f}s...")

    # Pre-generate LUT for simulated browser canvas construction
    try:
        import numpy as np
        has_numpy = True
        lut = np.zeros((512, 4), dtype=np.uint8)
        lut[:97] = [14, 22, 34, 150]
        lut[97:166] = [245, 215, 30, 130]
        lut[166:256] = [255, 0, 0, 255]
        lut[256:] = [255, 255, 0, 200]
    except ImportError:
        has_numpy = False

    intervals_ms: List[float] = []
    jitter_ms: List[float] = []
    parse_times_ms: List[float] = []
    decompress_times_ms: List[float] = []
    render_times_ms: List[float] = []
    total_client_times_ms: List[float] = []
    robot_latencies_ms: List[float] = []
    payload_sizes_bytes: List[int] = []
    time_steps: List[int] = []

    target_interval_ms = 1000.0 / target_hz
    t_start = time.perf_counter()
    t_last_arrival: Optional[float] = None
    sock.settimeout(3.5)

    try:
        while time.perf_counter() - t_start < duration_s:
            # 1. Read WebSocket Frame Header
            t_recv = None
            while len(buf) < 2:
                chunk = sock.recv(4096)
                if not chunk:
                    break
                buf += chunk
                t_recv = time.perf_counter()
            if len(buf) < 2:
                break

            b0, b1 = buf[0], buf[1]
            opcode = b0 & 0x0F
            plen = b1 & 0x7F
            idx = 2

            # Close frame received
            if opcode == 0x08:
                break
            # Ping frame -> reply Pong
            elif opcode == 0x09:
                pong = bytearray(buf[:idx])
                pong[0] = (pong[0] & 0xF0) | 0x0A
                sock.sendall(pong)
                buf = buf[idx:]
                continue

            if plen == 126:
                while len(buf) < 4:
                    chunk = sock.recv(4096)
                    if not chunk: break
                    buf += chunk
                    t_recv = time.perf_counter()
                plen = struct.unpack("!H", buf[2:4])[0]
                idx = 4
            elif plen == 127:
                while len(buf) < 10:
                    chunk = sock.recv(4096)
                    if not chunk: break
                    buf += chunk
                    t_recv = time.perf_counter()
                plen = struct.unpack("!Q", buf[2:10])[0]
                idx = 10

            while len(buf) < idx + plen:
                chunk = sock.recv(idx + plen - len(buf))
                if not chunk: break
                buf += chunk
                t_recv = time.perf_counter()

            payload = buf[idx : idx + plen]
            buf = buf[idx + plen :]

            # Record arrival timestamp
            t_arrival = t_recv if t_recv is not None else time.perf_counter()
            t_arrival_wall = time.time()
            if t_last_arrival is not None:
                dt_iv = (t_arrival - t_last_arrival) * 1000.0
                intervals_ms.append(dt_iv)
                jitter_ms.append(abs(dt_iv - target_interval_ms))
            t_last_arrival = t_arrival
            payload_sizes_bytes.append(len(payload))

            # 2. Emulate Browser Binary Unpacking & Decompression Pipeline
            t_p0 = time.perf_counter()
            decomp_ms = 0.0
            raster_ms = 0.0

            if len(payload) >= 232 and payload[:4] == b"FOLK":
                header_fmt = "<4s I I d 16f 4f 4f 16f 3f 2H 4f 5I"
                fields = struct.unpack(header_fmt, payload[:232])
                step_id = fields[2]
                ts_robot = fields[3]
                time_steps.append(step_id)

                # Approximate clock skew/robot-to-client delta
                dt_r2c = (t_arrival_wall - ts_robot) * 1000.0
                robot_latencies_ms.append(dt_r2c)

                # Unpack compression lengths
                prob_comp_len = fields[-5]
                prob_raw_len = fields[-4]
                haz_comp_len = fields[-3]
                meta_len = fields[-1]

                # Decompress costmaps if present (simulating DecompressionStream)
                t_d0 = time.perf_counter()
                if prob_comp_len > 0 and len(payload) >= 232 + prob_comp_len:
                    slice_prob = payload[232 : 232 + prob_comp_len]
                    if prob_comp_len < prob_raw_len:
                        try:
                            _ = zlib.decompress(slice_prob)
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                if haz_comp_len > 0:
                    slice_haz = payload[232 + prob_comp_len : 232 + prob_comp_len + haz_comp_len]
                    try:
                        _ = zlib.decompress(slice_haz)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                decomp_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t_d0) * 1000.0
                decompress_times_ms.append(decomp_ms)

                # Parse JSON metadata (trajectories, rays, etc.)
                if meta_len > 0:
                    meta_raw = payload[232 + prob_comp_len + haz_comp_len : 232 + prob_comp_len + haz_comp_len + meta_len]
                    try:
                        _ = json.loads(meta_raw.decode("utf-8"))
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                # 3. Emulate Browser 2D Canvas Rasterization (LUT mapping on costmap)
                t_r0 = time.perf_counter()
                if has_numpy:
                    dummy_prob = np.zeros((95, 125), dtype=np.uint8)
                    _ = lut[dummy_prob]
                raster_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t_r0) * 1000.0
                render_times_ms.append(raster_ms)

            parse_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t_p0) * 1000.0
            parse_times_ms.append(parse_ms)
            total_client_times_ms.append(parse_ms + raster_ms)

    except Exception as e:
        if verbose:
            print(f"[WebSocket Warning] Streaming ended: {e}")
    finally:
        sock.close()

    actual_duration_s = time.perf_counter() - t_start
    total_frames = len(payload_sizes_bytes)
    effective_hz = total_frames / max(0.001, actual_duration_s)

    # Frame drops (gaps in sequence IDs)
    drops = 0
    if len(time_steps) > 1:
        for i in range(1, len(time_steps)):
            gap = time_steps[i] - time_steps[i - 1]
            if gap > 3:  # 20 Hz decimation steps by 3
                drops += (gap // 3) - 1

    stutters = sum(1 for iv in intervals_ms if iv > 66.7)
    hitches = sum(1 for iv in intervals_ms if iv > 100.0)

    return {
        "connected": True,
        "mode": "websocket",
        "duration_s": actual_duration_s,
        "total_frames": total_frames,
        "effective_hz": effective_hz,
        "target_hz": target_hz,
        "drops": drops,
        "drop_rate_pct": (drops / max(1, total_frames + drops)) * 100.0,
        "stutters": stutters,
        "stutter_pct": (stutters / max(1, len(intervals_ms))) * 100.0,
        "hitches": hitches,
        "hitch_pct": (hitches / max(1, len(intervals_ms))) * 100.0,
        "intervals": compute_percentiles(intervals_ms),
        "jitter": compute_percentiles(jitter_ms),
        "robot_latencies": compute_percentiles(robot_latencies_ms),
        "parse": compute_percentiles(parse_times_ms),
        "decompress": compute_percentiles(decompress_times_ms),
        "render": compute_percentiles(render_times_ms),
        "total_client": compute_percentiles(total_client_times_ms),
        "payload_kb": (statistics.mean(payload_sizes_bytes) / 1024.0) if payload_sizes_bytes else 0.0,
    }


def benchmark_http_polling(
    base_url: str,
    duration_s: float = 6.0,
    target_hz: float = 20.0,
    verbose: bool = True,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Simulates the browser HTTP polling loop (/api/live_bin) across duration_s,
    measuring network Request-Response Round Trip Time (RTT), interval jitter,
    and client deserialization latency.
    """
    url_bin = f"{base_url}/api/live_bin"
    if verbose:
        print(f"\n[HTTP Profiler] Benchmarking HTTP Polling ({url_bin}) for {duration_s:.1f}s...")

    rtt_ms_list: List[float] = []
    intervals_ms: List[float] = []
    parse_ms_list: List[float] = []
    payload_sizes_bytes: List[int] = []

    target_dt_s = 1.0 / target_hz
    t_start = time.perf_counter()
    t_last_arrival: Optional[float] = None

    while time.perf_counter() - t_start < duration_s:
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        payload = b""
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(url_bin, headers={"User-Agent": "FolkraceLatencyTester/1.0"})
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=1.5) as resp:
                payload = resp.read()
            t_resp = time.perf_counter()
            rtt = (t_resp - t0) * 1000.0
            rtt_ms_list.append(rtt)
            payload_sizes_bytes.append(len(payload))

            if t_last_arrival is not None:
                intervals_ms.append((t_resp - t_last_arrival) * 1000.0)
            t_last_arrival = t_resp

            # Parse
            t_p0 = time.perf_counter()
            if len(payload) >= 232 and payload[:4] == b"FOLK":
                meta_len = struct.unpack("<I", payload[228:232])[0]
                if meta_len > 0 and len(payload) >= 232 + meta_len:
                    _ = json.loads(payload[232 : 232 + meta_len].decode("utf-8"))
            parse_ms_list.append((time.perf_counter() - t_p0) * 1000.0)

        except Exception as e:
            time.sleep(0.02)

        # Target interval rate sleep
        elapsed_s = time.perf_counter() - t0
        sleep_s = max(0.0, target_dt_s - elapsed_s)
        time.sleep(sleep_s)

    actual_duration_s = time.perf_counter() - t_start
    total_reqs = len(rtt_ms_list)
    effective_hz = total_reqs / max(0.001, actual_duration_s)

    stutters = sum(1 for iv in intervals_ms if iv > 66.7)
    hitches = sum(1 for iv in intervals_ms if iv > 100.0)

    return {
        "mode": "http_polling",
        "duration_s": actual_duration_s,
        "total_requests": total_reqs,
        "effective_hz": effective_hz,
        "stutters": stutters,
        "stutter_pct": (stutters / max(1, len(intervals_ms))) * 100.0,
        "hitches": hitches,
        "hitch_pct": (hitches / max(1, len(intervals_ms))) * 100.0,
        "rtt": compute_percentiles(rtt_ms_list),
        "intervals": compute_percentiles(intervals_ms),
        "parse": compute_percentiles(parse_ms_list),
        "payload_kb": (statistics.mean(payload_sizes_bytes) / 1024.0) if payload_sizes_bytes else 0.0,
    }


def find_firefox_executable() -> Optional[str]:
    """Finds installed Firefox browser binary."""
    for p in FIREFOX_PATHS:
        if os.path.exists(p):
            return p
    return None


def run_firefox_live_probe(base_url: str, duration_s: float = 6.0) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Launches Firefox with an automated diagnostic test page to measure real
    DOM performance.now() metrics directly inside the browser engine.
    """
    ff_path = find_firefox_executable()
    if not ff_path:
        return {"error": "Mozilla Firefox not found on system"}

    print(f"\n[Firefox Harness] Found Firefox: {ff_path}")
    print(f"[Firefox Harness] Opening Tactical Mission Control: {base_url}")
    print(f"[Firefox Harness] Inspecting real-time browser performance metrics for {duration_s:.1f}s...")

    # We open Firefox directly to the UI
    try:
        proc = subprocess.Popen([ff_path, base_url])
        time.sleep(duration_s)
        # We leave browser open for user or report status
        return {
            "browser": "Firefox",
            "path": ff_path,
            "url": base_url,
            "status": "Running interactively",
            "note": "Examine live Performance HUD at top right of Tactical Mission Control."
        }
    except Exception as e:
        return {"error": str(e)}


def print_comparison_report(ws_res: Dict[str, Any], http_res: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None):
    """Prints a clean ASCII comparison table highlighting client latency percentiles."""
    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print("  FOLKRACE CLIENT BROWSER LATENCY REPORT (End-to-End Flight to Screen)")
    print("=" * 80)

    if ws_res.get("connected"):
        print(f"  Transport Mechanism     : WebSocket Streaming (ws://.../ws_live)")
        print(f"  Test Duration           : {ws_res['duration_s']:.2f}s ({ws_res['total_frames']} frames captured)")
        print(f"  Effective Frame Rate    : {ws_res['effective_hz']:.1f} Hz (Target: {ws_res['target_hz']} Hz)")
        print(f"  Frame Drop Rate         : {ws_res['drop_rate_pct']:.2f}% ({ws_res['drops']} dropped)")
        print(f"  Average Payload Size    : {ws_res['payload_kb']:.2f} KB/frame (Header + Trajectories + Costmaps)")
        print("-" * 80)
        print(f"  {'METRIC (WebSocket)':<26} | {'MEAN':>7} | {'P50':>7} | {'P90':>7} | {'P95':>7} | {'P99':>7} | {'P99.9':>7} | {'MAX':>7}")
        print("-" * 80)

        def print_row(label, d):
            print(f"  {label:<26} | {d['mean']:6.2f}ms | {d['p50']:6.2f}ms | {d['p90']:6.2f}ms | {d['p95']:6.2f}ms | {d['p99']:6.2f}ms | {d['p999']:6.2f}ms | {d['max']:6.2f}ms")

        print_row("Packet Arrival Interval", ws_res["intervals"])
        print_row("Arrival Jitter (|dt - 50|)", ws_res["jitter"])
        if ws_res.get("robot_latencies") and ws_res["robot_latencies"]["count"] > 0:
            print_row("Robot Age at Reception", ws_res["robot_latencies"])
        print_row("Client Unpack & JSON", ws_res["parse"])
        print_row("Client Canvas / LUT", ws_res["render"])
        print_row("Total Client Processing", ws_res["total_client"])
        print("-" * 80)
        print(f"  Stutter Violations (>66.7ms) : {ws_res['stutters']} ({ws_res['stutter_pct']:.1f}%)")
        print(f"  Hitch Violations (>100.0ms)  : {ws_res['hitches']} ({ws_res['hitch_pct']:.1f}%)")
    else:
        print(f"  WebSocket Test Failed: {ws_res.get('error')}")

    if http_res and http_res.get("rtt"):
        print("\n" + "-" * 80)
        print(f"  COMPARISON: HTTP Polling Fallback (/api/live_bin)")
        print("-" * 80)
        print(f"  {'METRIC (HTTP Polling)':<26} | {'MEAN':>7} | {'P50':>7} | {'P90':>7} | {'P95':>7} | {'P99':>7} | {'P99.9':>7} | {'MAX':>7}")
        print("-" * 80)
        print_row("Request RTT (Round Trip)", http_res["rtt"])
        print_row("Inter-frame Delivery", http_res["intervals"])
        print("-" * 80)
        print(f"  HTTP Stutters (>66.7ms)     : {http_res['stutters']} ({http_res['stutter_pct']:.1f}%)")
        print(f"  HTTP Hitches (>100.0ms)      : {http_res['hitches']} ({http_res['hitch_pct']:.1f}%)")

    # Verdict
    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print("  VERDICT & BROWSER USER EXPERIENCE SUMMARY:")
    if ws_res.get("connected"):
        iv = ws_res["intervals"]
        p99_ms = iv["p99"]
        p999_ms = iv["p999"]
        stutter_pct = ws_res["stutter_pct"]

        if p999_ms <= 65.0 and stutter_pct <= 2.0:
            print("  >>> [EXCELLENT] Smooth 20 Hz Telemetry Stream (P99.9 < 65ms, zero visual hitches).")
        elif p99_ms <= 75.0 and stutter_pct <= 10.0:
            print("  >>> [GOOD] Consistent streaming with minor Wi-Fi jitter (P99 < 75ms).")
        else:
            print("  >>> [WARNING] Noticeable Wi-Fi delivery jitter or packet bursts detected.")

    print("=" * 80 + "\n")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Folkrace Browser Latency & Telemetry Profiler")
    parser.add_argument("--host", default=None, help="Target Pi 4 IP or hostname (auto-detects if omitted)")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=DEFAULT_PORT, help="Port of Pi debug server (default: 8080)")
    parser.add_argument("--duration", type=float, default=8.0, help="Benchmark duration in seconds (default: 8.0s)")
    parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=["ws", "http", "all", "firefox"], default="all", help="Benchmark mode")
    parser.add_argument("--firefox", action="store_true", help="Launch Firefox and open live dashboard")
    parser.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output raw JSON results")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    active_host, base_url = resolve_host(args.host, args.port)

    if args.firefox or args.mode == "firefox":
        run_firefox_live_probe(base_url, duration_s=args.duration)
        return

    ws_res = {}
    http_res = None

    if args.mode in ("ws", "all"):
        ws_res = benchmark_websocket_stream(
            host=active_host,
            port=args.port,
            duration_s=args.duration,
            verbose=not args.json
        )

    if args.mode in ("http", "all"):
        http_res = benchmark_http_polling(
            base_url=base_url,
            duration_s=min(5.0, args.duration),
            verbose=not args.json
        )

    if args.json:
        report = {"websocket": ws_res, "http": http_res}
        print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    else:
        print_comparison_report(ws_res, http_res)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== FILE: tools/optimize_wrong_way_params.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
================================================================================
  PARALLEL HEADLESS WEBOTS WRONG-WAY & RECOVERY PARAMETER OPTIMIZER
================================================================================
  Uses an elitist (mu + lambda) evolutionary strategy across 4 parallel
  headless Webots workers to optimize AntiWrongWayWatchdog & StuckRecoveryManager
  parameters across all 12 scenarios in test_wrong_way.wbt and track_racing.wbt.
================================================================================
"""

import argparse
import concurrent.futures
import json
import os
import random
import sys
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple

TOOLS_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if TOOLS_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, TOOLS_DIR)

from run_webots_wrong_way_tests import run_world_eval

BOUNDS: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float, bool]] = {
    "aww_duration_scale": (0.25, 0.50, False),
    "aww_max_detect_dist_m": (1.80, 2.35, False),
    "aww_max_lat_xv_m": (0.42, 0.65, False),
    "aww_min_confirmed": (3, 4, True),
    "aww_confirm_time_s": (0.032, 0.085, False),
    "aww_post_cooldown_s": (0.20, 0.65, False),
    "rec_180_speed_mps": (0.60, 1.05, False),
    "rec_180_rev_clear_m": (0.32, 0.55, False),
    "rec_180_rev_min_s": (0.12, 0.40, False),
    "rec_180_align_deg": (24.0, 38.0, False),
}

BASELINE: Dict[str, Any] = {
    "aww_duration_scale": 0.35,
    "aww_max_detect_dist_m": 2.20,
    "aww_max_lat_xv_m": 0.55,
    "aww_min_confirmed": 3,
    "aww_confirm_time_s": 0.048,
    "aww_post_cooldown_s": 0.35,
    "rec_180_speed_mps": 0.85,
    "rec_180_rev_clear_m": 0.38,
    "rec_180_rev_min_s": 0.18,
    "rec_180_align_deg": 30.0,
}


def clamp_params(p: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    out: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for k, (lo, hi, is_int) in BOUNDS.items():
        val = max(lo, min(hi, float(p[k])))
        out[k] = int(round(val)) if is_int else round(val, 4)
    return out


def mutate_params(parent: Dict[str, Any], sigma: float, rng: random.Random) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    child: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    for k, (lo, hi, is_int) in BOUNDS.items():
        span = hi - lo
        if is_int:
            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                child[k] = parent[k] + rng.choice([-1, 1])
            else:
                child[k] = parent[k]
        else:
            child[k] = float(parent[k]) + rng.gauss(0.0, sigma * span)
    return clamp_params(child)


def score_results(res_ww: dict, res_tr: dict) -> Tuple[float, int, int, List[str]]:
    all_r = list(res_ww.get("results", [])) + list(res_tr.get("results", []))
    total = len(all_r)
    passed_cnt = sum(1 for r in all_r if r.get("passed"))
    failed_names = [r.get("name", "?") for r in all_r if not r.get("passed")]

    fitness = 1000.0
    det_times: List[float] = []
    rec_times: List[float] = []
    post_dists: List[float] = []
    clean_prog = 0.0

    for r in all_r:
        if r.get("flipped"):
            fitness -= 5000.0
        if r.get("off_track") and r.get("name") != "T2_clean_rev_opponents_25s":
            fitness -= 5000.0
        if "ww_triggers" in r:
            # Clean scenario
            ww = int(r.get("ww_fp", r.get("ww_triggers", 0)))
            fitness -= 10000.0 * ww
            clean_prog += float(r.get("progress_m", 0.0))
            if not r.get("passed"):
                fitness -= 2000.0
        else:
            # Spin scenario
            fitness -= 10000.0 * int(r.get("pre_ww", 0))
            fitness -= 1000.0 * int(r.get("retriggers", 0))
            if not r.get("passed"):
                fitness -= 2000.0
            if r.get("detect_s") is not None:
                det_times.append(float(r["detect_s"]))
            else:
                det_times.append(2.0)
            if r.get("rec_done_s") is not None:
                rec_times.append(float(r["rec_done_s"]))
            else:
                rec_times.append(4.5)
            post_dists.append(float(r.get("post_rec_dist_m", 0.0)))

    if det_times:
        fitness -= 250.0 * (sum(det_times) / len(det_times))
    if rec_times:
        fitness -= 150.0 * (sum(rec_times) / len(rec_times))
        fitness -= 80.0 * max(rec_times)
    if post_dists:
        fitness += 20.0 * (sum(post_dists) / len(post_dists))
    fitness += 3.0 * clean_prog

    return round(fitness, 2), passed_cnt, total, failed_names


def evaluate_candidate(cand_id: int, params: Dict[str, Any], worker_slot: int) -> Tuple[float, int, int, List[str], Dict[str, Any]]:
    p_json = json.dumps(params)
    port_base = 10400 + worker_slot * 10
    res_ww = run_world_eval("test_wrong_way", "all", False, 180.0, params_json=p_json, port=port_base)
    res_tr = run_world_eval("track_racing", "all", False, 420.0, params_json=p_json, port=port_base + 1)
    fit, p_cnt, tot, failed = score_results(res_ww, res_tr)
    return fit, p_cnt, tot, failed, params


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Optimize wrong-way parameters in parallel headless Webots.")
    parser.add_argument("--gens", type=int, default=4, help="Number of generations")
    parser.add_argument("--pop", type=int, default=6, help="Candidates per generation")
    parser.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=2, help="Parallel Webots workers")
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    rng = random.Random(args.seed)
    t_start = time.perf_counter()

    population: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [dict(BASELINE)]
    while len(population) < args.pop:
        population.append(mutate_params(BASELINE, sigma=0.22, rng=rng))

    best_ever = None  # (fit, p_cnt, tot, failed, params)

    print("=" * 80)
    print(f"  PARALLEL WEBOTS WRONG-WAY OPTIMIZER (gens={args.gens}, pop={args.pop}, workers={args.workers})")
    print("=" * 80)

    for gen in range(args.gens):
        t_gen = time.perf_counter()
        scored: List[Tuple[float, int, int, List[str], Dict[str, Any]]] = []
        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.workers) as pool:
            futs = [
                pool.submit(evaluate_candidate, idx, cand, idx % args.workers)
                for idx, cand in enumerate(population)
            ]
            for fut in concurrent.futures.as_completed(futs):
                scored.append(fut.result())

        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
        top = scored[0]
        if best_ever is None or top[0] > best_ever[0]:
            best_ever = top

        print(
            f"[Gen {gen+1}/{args.gens}] Best={top[0]:.2f} ({top[1]}/{top[2]} PASS, fail={top[3]}) "
            f"| EverBest={best_ever[0]:.2f} ({best_ever[1]}/{best_ever[2]} PASS) "
            f"| {time.perf_counter()-t_gen:.1f}s",
            flush=True,
        )

        # Elitist selection (top 2) + mutated offspring
        elites = [scored[0][4], scored[min(1, len(scored) - 1)][4]]
        sigma = max(0.08, 0.22 * (0.75 ** (gen + 1)))
        next_pop = [dict(elites[0])]
        while len(next_pop) < args.pop:
            parent = rng.choice(elites)
            next_pop.append(mutate_params(parent, sigma=sigma, rng=rng))
        population = next_pop

    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print(f"  OPTIMIZATION COMPLETE in {time.perf_counter()-t_start:.1f}s")
    print(f"  Best Fitness: {best_ever[0]:.2f} ({best_ever[1]}/{best_ever[2]} PASS, failed={best_ever[3]})")
    print("  Best Params JSON:")
    print(json.dumps(best_ever[4], indent=2))
    print("=" * 80)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


=== FILE: tools/run_webots_wrong_way_tests.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
================================================================================
  AUTOMATED HEADLESS WEBOTS 3D WRONG-WAY & RECOVERY TEST RUNNER
================================================================================
  Launches headless Webots against test_wrong_way.wbt and track_racing.wbt
  using WEBOTS_CONTROLLER_MODE=wrong_way_eval, collects structured JSON results,
  and prints a pass/fail summary table.
================================================================================
"""

import argparse
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from typing import Optional
import uuid

REPO_ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
WEBOTS_DIR = os.path.join(REPO_ROOT, "5_webots")
MY_PROJECT_DIR = os.path.join(WEBOTS_DIR, "simulation", "my_project")
PLANNERS_DIR = os.path.join(MY_PROJECT_DIR, "controllers", "tof_path_planner")
GA_DIR = os.path.join(WEBOTS_DIR, "optimization", "ga_planner_optimizer")
WEBOTS_BIN = r"C:\Program Files\Webots\msys64\mingw64\bin\webots.exe"

WORLDS = {
    "test_wrong_way": os.path.join(MY_PROJECT_DIR, "worlds", "test_wrong_way.wbt"),
    "track_racing": os.path.join(MY_PROJECT_DIR, "worlds", "track_racing.wbt"),
}


def run_world_eval(
    world_key: str,
    scenarios: str,
    render: bool,
    timeout_s: float,
    params_json: str = "",
    port: Optional[int] = None,
) -> dict:
    world_path = WORLDS[world_key]
    if not os.path.isfile(world_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"World not found: {world_path}")
    if not os.path.isfile(WEBOTS_BIN):
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Webots executable not found: {WEBOTS_BIN}")

    scratch_dir = os.path.join(REPO_ROOT, "scratch")
    os.makedirs(scratch_dir, exist_ok=True)
    out_json = os.path.join(scratch_dir, f"aww_eval_{world_key}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.json")

    env = os.environ.copy()
    webots_home = r"C:\Program Files\Webots"
    webots_bin_dir = os.path.dirname(WEBOTS_BIN)
    py_dir = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
    py_scripts = os.path.join(py_dir, "Scripts")
    env["WEBOTS_HOME"] = webots_home
    env["PATH"] = webots_bin_dir + os.pathsep + py_dir + os.pathsep + py_scripts + os.pathsep + env.get("PATH", "")
    env["PYTHONPATH"] = os.pathsep.join([
        PLANNERS_DIR,
        os.path.join(GA_DIR, "data"),
        GA_DIR,
        MY_PROJECT_DIR,
        REPO_ROOT,
    ])
    env["WEBOTS_CONTROLLER_MODE"] = "wrong_way_eval"
    env["AWW_EVAL_WORLD"] = world_key
    env["AWW_EVAL_SCENARIOS"] = scenarios
    env["AWW_EVAL_OUT"] = out_json
    if params_json:
        env["AWW_EVAL_PARAMS_JSON"] = params_json
    env["PYTHONUNBUFFERED"] = "1"

    port_arg = [f"--port={port}"] if port is not None else []
    if render:
        cmd = [WEBOTS_BIN, *port_arg, "--mode=realtime", "--batch", "--stdout", "--stderr", world_path]
        startupinfo = None
        creationflags = 0
    else:
        cmd = [
            WEBOTS_BIN,
            *port_arg,
            "--mode=fast",
            "--no-rendering",
            "--batch",
            "--minimize",
            "--stdout",
            "--stderr",
            world_path,
        ]
        startupinfo = None
        creationflags = 0
        if sys.platform == "win32":
            startupinfo = subprocess.STARTUPINFO()
            startupinfo.dwFlags |= subprocess.STARTF_USESHOWWINDOW
            startupinfo.wShowWindow = 0
            creationflags = getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0x08000000)

    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    proc = subprocess.Popen(
        cmd,
        env=env,
        cwd=MY_PROJECT_DIR,
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
        stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        text=True,
        startupinfo=startupinfo,
        creationflags=creationflags,
    )
    try:
        stdout_txt, _ = proc.communicate(timeout=timeout_s)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        if sys.platform == "win32":
            subprocess.run(["taskkill", "/F", "/T", "/PID", str(proc.pid)], stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        else:
            proc.kill()
        stdout_txt, _ = proc.communicate()
        return {
            "world": world_key,
            "all_passed": False,
            "results": [{"name": f"{world_key}_timeout", "passed": False, "error": f"Timed out after {timeout_s}s"}],
            "stdout": stdout_txt[-1500:],
        }
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(out_json):
            try:
                with open(out_json, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
            except Exception as e:
                data = {"world": world_key, "all_passed": False, "results": [], "error": str(e)}
            if not data.get("all_passed", False) and stdout_txt:
                data["stdout"] = stdout_txt[-2500:]
            try:
                os.remove(out_json)
            except OSError:
                pass
        else:
            data = {
                "world": world_key,
                "all_passed": False,
                "results": [{"name": f"{world_key}_no_output", "passed": False, "rc": proc.returncode}],
                "stdout": stdout_txt[-1500:] if stdout_txt else "",
            }

    data["elapsed_s"] = round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 2)
    return data


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run automated Webots 3D wrong-way & recovery tests.")
    parser.add_argument("--world", choices=["test_wrong_way", "track_racing", "all"], default="all")
    parser.add_argument("--scenario", default="all", help="Comma-separated scenario names or 'all'")
    parser.add_argument("--render", action="store_true", help="Launch Webots in real-time rendered mode")
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=240.0, help="Per-world wall-clock timeout in seconds")
    parser.add_argument("--params-json", default="", help="Optional JSON string of parameter overrides")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=None, help="Optional Webots port")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    world_list = ["test_wrong_way", "track_racing"] if args.world == "all" else [args.world]

    print("=" * 80)
    print("  HEADLESS WEBOTS 3D WRONG-WAY & RECOVERY TEST SUITE")
    print("=" * 80)

    overall_ok = True
    for wk in world_list:
        print(f"\n--- World: {wk} ---")
        res = run_world_eval(wk, args.scenario, args.render, args.timeout, params_json=args.params_json, port=args.port)
        for r in res.get("results", []):
            tag = "PASS" if r.get("passed") else "FAIL"
            detail = {k: v for k, v in r.items() if k not in ("name", "passed")}
            print(f"  [{tag}] {r.get('name', '?'):30s} | {detail}")
        if not res.get("all_passed", False):
            overall_ok = False
            if res.get("stdout"):
                print("  [Webots tail stdout]:\n" + res["stdout"])
        print(f"  World elapsed: {res.get('elapsed_s', '?')}s")

    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print(f"  OVERALL RESULT: {'ALL PASSED' if overall_ok else 'FAILED'}")
    print("=" * 80)
    return 0 if overall_ok else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


=== FILE: tools/stm32_foc_trace_test.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run a high-rate STM32 FOC trace while the ESP32 commands the motors."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import math
import statistics
import struct
import time
from pathlib import Path

import serial


TRACE_HEADER = struct.Struct("<2sHHHII")
TRACE_SAMPLE = struct.Struct("<HHhhhhhh")
TRACE_SYNC = b"FT"
MOTOR_POLE_PAIRS = 11
RAW_COUNTS = 16384


def read_trace(port: serial.Serial) -> tuple[dict[str, int], list[tuple[int, ...]]]:
    data = bytearray()
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 8.0
    expected = None
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        chunk = port.read(4096)
        if chunk:
            data.extend(chunk)
        start = data.find(TRACE_SYNC)
        if start >= 0 and len(data) >= start + TRACE_HEADER.size:
            header = TRACE_HEADER.unpack_from(data, start)
            count = header[3]
            expected = start + TRACE_HEADER.size + count * TRACE_SAMPLE.size
            if len(data) >= expected:
                payload = data[start + TRACE_HEADER.size : expected]
                samples = [
                    TRACE_SAMPLE.unpack_from(payload, offset)
                    for offset in range(0, len(payload), TRACE_SAMPLE.size)
                ]
                return (
                    {
                        "version": header[1],
                        "sample_hz": header[2],
                        "sample_count": count,
                        "overwritten": header[4],
                    },
                    samples,
                )
    raise TimeoutError(f"trace dump incomplete; expected_end={expected}, received={len(data)}")


def unwrap_delta(delta: int) -> int:
    half = RAW_COUNTS // 2
    return ((delta + half) % RAW_COUNTS) - half


def motor_analysis(samples: list[tuple[int, ...]], motor_index: int) -> dict[str, object]:
    raw_index = motor_index
    target_index = 2 + motor_index
    actual_index = 4 + motor_index
    uq_index = 6 + motor_index
    raw = [sample[raw_index] for sample in samples]
    deltas = [unwrap_delta(b - a) for a, b in zip(raw, raw[1:])]
    electrical_steps = [
        delta * (2.0 * math.pi * MOTOR_POLE_PAIRS / RAW_COUNTS) for delta in deltas
    ]

    actual = [sample[actual_index] for sample in samples]
    target = [sample[target_index] for sample in samples]
    uq = [sample[uq_index] for sample in samples]
    actual_steps = [abs(b - a) for a, b in zip(actual, actual[1:])]
    phase_jumps = [abs(step) for step in electrical_steps]
    phase_tracking_errors = [
        measured - (
            target[index]
            * (2.0 * math.pi / 60.0)
            * MOTOR_POLE_PAIRS
            / 500.0
        )
        for index, measured in enumerate(electrical_steps)
    ]
    return {
        "target_rpm_avg": round(statistics.mean(target), 1),
        "actual_rpm_avg": round(statistics.mean(actual), 1),
        "actual_rpm_minmax": [min(actual), max(actual)],
        "uq_mv_max_abs": max(abs(value) for value in uq),
        "max_raw_delta_counts": max(abs(value) for value in deltas),
        "max_electrical_step_rad": round(max(phase_jumps), 3),
        "electrical_steps_over_pi": sum(value > math.pi for value in phase_jumps),
        "max_phase_tracking_error_rad": round(max(abs(value) for value in phase_tracking_errors), 3),
        "phase_tracking_errors_over_1rad": sum(
            abs(value) > 1.0 for value in phase_tracking_errors
        ),
        "actual_steps_over_40rpm": sum(value > 40 for value in actual_steps),
    }


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--stm-port", default="COM13")
    parser.add_argument("--esp-port", default="COM5")
    parser.add_argument("--duration", type=float, default=10.0)
    parser.add_argument("--speed", type=float, default=-0.8)
    parser.add_argument("--steer-angle", type=int, default=90)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=Path("stm32_foc_trace.bin"))
    args = parser.parse_args()

    with serial.Serial(args.stm_port, 921600, timeout=0.01) as stm, serial.Serial(
        args.esp_port, 921600, timeout=0.005
    ) as esp:
        stm.reset_input_buffer()
        esp.reset_input_buffer()
        stm.write(b"TRACE_START\n")
        stm.flush()
        time.sleep(0.1)
        try:
            for command in (
                b"MOTORS\n",
                f"A{args.steer_angle}\n".encode(),
                f"V {args.speed}\n".encode(),
            ):
                esp.write(command)
                esp.flush()
                time.sleep(0.15)
            time.sleep(args.duration)
        finally:
            esp.write(b"ESTOP\n")
            esp.flush()
            time.sleep(0.5)
            stm.write(b"TRACE_STOP\n")
            stm.flush()
            time.sleep(0.1)
            stm.reset_input_buffer()
            stm.write(b"TRACE_DUMP\n")
            stm.flush()
        header, samples = read_trace(stm)

    raw = b"".join(TRACE_SAMPLE.pack(*sample) for sample in samples)
    args.output.write_bytes(TRACE_HEADER.pack(TRACE_SYNC, header["version"], header["sample_hz"], header["sample_count"], header["overwritten"], 0) + raw)
    result = {
        "trace_file": str(args.output),
        "header": header,
        "motor1": motor_analysis(samples, 0),
        "motor2": motor_analysis(samples, 1),
    }
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== FILE: tools/sync_params.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
================================================================================
  FOLKRACE PARAMETER SYNCHRONIZATION PIPELINE
================================================================================
  Single Source of Truth: brain/vehicle_config.json
  Generates:
    1. brain/vehicle_params.py                     (Webots, Pi 4, Launchers)
    2. 3_esp32/ESP32_Main/include/vehicle_params.h (ESP32 C++ Firmware)
================================================================================
"""

import os
import sys
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path

# Determine project repository root (supports CLI and PlatformIO SCons context)
try:
    SCRIPT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    REPO_ROOT = SCRIPT_DIR.parent
except NameError:
    # In PlatformIO SCons execution, __file__ is undefined
    REPO_ROOT = Path.cwd()
    SCRIPT_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "tools"

CONFIG_PATH = REPO_ROOT / "brain" / "vehicle_config.json"
PY_OUT_PATH = REPO_ROOT / "brain" / "vehicle_params.py"
H_OUT_PATH = REPO_ROOT / "3_esp32" / "ESP32_Main" / "include" / "vehicle_params.h"


def load_config() -> dict:
    if not CONFIG_PATH.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Configuration file not found: {CONFIG_PATH}")
    with open(CONFIG_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return json.load(f)


def compute_derived_params(cfg: dict) -> dict:
    v = cfg["vehicle"]
    d = cfg["dynamics"]
    t = cfg["timing"]

    rear_offset = float(v["rear_axle_offset_m"])
    front_offset = float(v["front_axle_offset_m"])
    wheelbase = front_offset - rear_offset

    tire_mu = float(d["tire_mu"])
    gravity_g = float(d["gravity_g"])
    a_max_base = tire_mu * gravity_g

    accel_fwd_max = float(d["accel_fwd_ratio"]) * a_max_base
    brake_decel_max = float(d["brake_decel_ratio"]) * a_max_base
    lat_accel_max = float(d["lat_accel_ratio"]) * a_max_base

    max_steer_rad = float(v["max_steering_rad"])
    r_min_turn = wheelbase / math.tan(max_steer_rad)
    v_min_cruise = math.sqrt(lat_accel_max * r_min_turn)

    ctrl_freq = float(t["control_loop_freq_hz"])
    ctrl_period_us = int(1_000_000 / ctrl_freq)
    ctrl_dt_s = 1.0 / ctrl_freq

    return {
        "wheelbase_m": wheelbase,
        "a_max_base": a_max_base,
        "accel_fwd_max": accel_fwd_max,
        "brake_decel_max": brake_decel_max,
        "lat_accel_max": lat_accel_max,
        "r_min_turn": r_min_turn,
        "v_min_cruise": v_min_cruise,
        "ctrl_period_us": ctrl_period_us,
        "ctrl_dt_s": ctrl_dt_s,
    }


def generate_python(cfg: dict, derived: dict) -> str:
    v = cfg["vehicle"]
    d = cfg["dynamics"]
    s = cfg["servo"]
    t = cfg["timing"]
    c = cfg["costmap"]

    lines = [
        '"""',
        'AUTO-GENERATED FILE -- DO NOT EDIT DIRECTLY.',
        'Generated by tools/sync_params.py from brain/vehicle_config.json',
        '"""',
        'import math',
        '',
        '# ============================================================================== #',
        '# 1. PHYSICAL VEHICLE & ACTUATOR SPECIFICATIONS                                  #',
        '# ============================================================================== #',
        f'REAR_AXLE_OFFSET_M: float = {v["rear_axle_offset_m"]}',
        f'FRONT_AXLE_OFFSET_M: float = {v["front_axle_offset_m"]}',
        f'WHEELBASE: float = {derived["wheelbase_m"]}',
        f'TRACK_REAR: float = {v["track_rear_m"]}',
        f'TRACK_FRONT: float = {v["track_front_m"]}',
        f'TRACK_KINGPIN: float = {v["track_kingpin_m"]}',
        f'WHEEL_RADIUS: float = {v["wheel_radius_m"]}',
        f'VEHICLE_MASS: float = {v["vehicle_mass_kg"]}',
        '',
        f'ACKERMANN_PERCENT: float = {v["ackermann_percent"]}',
        f'MAX_SERVO_RAD: float = {v["max_servo_rad"]}',
        f'MAX_STEERING_RAD: float = {v["max_steering_rad"]}',
        f'SERVO_MAX_SPEED_RAD_S: float = {v["servo_max_speed_rad_s"]}',
        '',
        f'MOTOR_DIR: float = {v["motor_dir"]}',
        f'V_MAX_WHEEL: float = {v["v_max_wheel_mps"]}',
        f'V_MAX_CRUISE: float = {v["v_max_cruise_mps"]}',
        '',
        '# ============================================================================== #',
        '# 2. TIRE & VEHICLE DYNAMICS LIMITS                                              #',
        '# ============================================================================== #',
        f'TIRE_MU: float = {d["tire_mu"]}',
        f'GRAVITY_G: float = {d["gravity_g"]}',
        f'A_MAX_BASE: float = {derived["a_max_base"]}',
        f'ACCEL_FWD_RATIO: float = {d["accel_fwd_ratio"]}',
        f'BRAKE_DECEL_RATIO: float = {d["brake_decel_ratio"]}',
        f'LAT_ACCEL_RATIO: float = {d["lat_accel_ratio"]}',
        f'ACCEL_FWD_MAX: float = {derived["accel_fwd_max"]}',
        f'BRAKE_DECEL_MAX: float = {derived["brake_decel_max"]}',
        f'LAT_ACCEL_MAX: float = {derived["lat_accel_max"]}',
        f'R_MIN_TURN: float = {derived["r_min_turn"]}',
        f'V_MIN_CRUISE: float = {derived["v_min_cruise"]}',
        f'SLIP_GATE_SPEED_MPS: float = {d["slip_gate_speed_mps"]}',
        f'SLIP_THRESH_SPIN_RAD_S: float = {d["slip_thresh_spin_rad_s"]}',
        '',
        '# ============================================================================== #',
        '# 3. DIGITAL STEERING SERVO PWM CONFIGURATION                                    #',
        '# ============================================================================== #',
        f'SERVO_PWM_PIN: int = {s["pwm_pin"]}',
        f'SERVO_PWM_CHANNEL: int = {s["pwm_channel"]}',
        f'SERVO_PWM_FREQ_HZ: int = {s["pwm_freq_hz"]}',
        f'SERVO_PWM_RESOLUTION: int = {s["pwm_resolution"]}',
        f'SERVO_PULSE_MIN_US: float = {s["pulse_min_us"]}',
        f'SERVO_PULSE_NEUTRAL_US: float = {s["pulse_neutral_us"]}',
        f'SERVO_PULSE_MAX_US: float = {s["pulse_max_us"]}',
        '',
        '# ============================================================================== #',
        '# 4. TIMING & FREQUENCIES                                                        #',
        '# ============================================================================== #',
        f'TOF_HZ: float = {t["tof_hz"]}',
        f'PI_LOOP_HZ: float = {t["pi_loop_hz"]}',
        f'DT_PI_LOOP: float = {1.0 / t["pi_loop_hz"]}',
        f'CONTROL_LOOP_FREQ_HZ: int = {t["control_loop_freq_hz"]}',
        f'CONTROL_LOOP_DT_S: float = {derived["ctrl_dt_s"]}',
        f'SIM_TIME_STEP_MS: float = {t["sim_time_step_ms"]}',
        f'UART_BAUD: int = {t["uart_baud"]}',
        '',
        '# ============================================================================== #',
        '# 5. COSTMAP BOUNDS & RESOLUTION                                                 #',
        '# ============================================================================== #',
        f'GRID_X_MAX: float = {c["grid_x_max"]}',
        f'GRID_X_MIN: float = {c["grid_x_min"]}',
        f'GRID_Y_MIN: float = {c["grid_y_min"]}',
        f'GRID_Y_MAX: float = {c["grid_y_max"]}',
        f'GRID_RES: float = {c["grid_res"]}',
        f'R_INFLATION_M: float = {c["r_inflation_m"]}',
        '',
    ]
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def generate_c_header(cfg: dict, derived: dict) -> str:
    v = cfg["vehicle"]
    d = cfg["dynamics"]
    s = cfg["servo"]
    t = cfg["timing"]
    c = cfg["costmap"]

    lines = [
        '#ifndef VEHICLE_PARAMS_H',
        '#define VEHICLE_PARAMS_H',
        '',
        '// =============================================================================',
        '// AUTO-GENERATED FILE -- DO NOT EDIT DIRECTLY.',
        '// Generated by tools/sync_params.py from brain/vehicle_config.json',
        '// =============================================================================',
        '',
        '// --- Vehicle Dimensions & Geometry ---',
        f'#define VEHICLE_REAR_AXLE_OFFSET_M  {v["rear_axle_offset_m"]:.4f}f',
        f'#define VEHICLE_FRONT_AXLE_OFFSET_M {v["front_axle_offset_m"]:.4f}f',
        f'#define VEHICLE_WHEELBASE_M         {derived["wheelbase_m"]:.4f}f',
        f'#define VEHICLE_TRACK_REAR_M        {v["track_rear_m"]:.4f}f',
        f'#define VEHICLE_TRACK_FRONT_M       {v["track_front_m"]:.4f}f',
        f'#define VEHICLE_TRACK_KINGPIN_M     {v["track_kingpin_m"]:.4f}f',
        f'#define VEHICLE_WHEEL_RADIUS_M      {v["wheel_radius_m"]:.4f}f',
        f'#define VEHICLE_MASS_KG             {v["vehicle_mass_kg"]:.3f}f',
        '',
        '// --- Steering Kinematics & Limits ---',
        f'#define VEHICLE_ACKERMANN_PCT       {v["ackermann_percent"]:.2f}f',
        f'#define VEHICLE_MAX_SERVO_RAD       {v["max_servo_rad"]:.6f}f',
        f'#define VEHICLE_MAX_STEER_RAD       {v["max_steering_rad"]:.6f}f',
        f'#define VEHICLE_SERVO_MAX_RATE      {v["servo_max_speed_rad_s"]:.2f}f',
        f'#define VEHICLE_MOTOR_DIR           {v["motor_dir"]:.1f}f',
        f'#define VEHICLE_MAX_WHEEL_SPEED     {v["v_max_wheel_mps"]:.2f}f',
        '',
        '// --- Tire Friction & Dynamic Traction Envelopes ---',
        f'#define DEFAULT_ROAD_MU             {d["tire_mu"]:.2f}f',
        f'#define GRAVITY_G_CONST             {d["gravity_g"]:.5f}f',
        f'#define ACCEL_FWD_RATIO             {d["accel_fwd_ratio"]:.2f}f',
        f'#define BRAKE_DECEL_RATIO           {d["brake_decel_ratio"]:.2f}f',
        f'#define LAT_ACCEL_RATIO             {d["lat_accel_ratio"]:.2f}f',
        f'#define ACCEL_FWD_MAX               {derived["accel_fwd_max"]:.3f}f',
        f'#define BRAKE_DECEL_MAX             {derived["brake_decel_max"]:.3f}f',
        f'#define LAT_ACCEL_MAX               {derived["lat_accel_max"]:.3f}f',
        f'#define R_MIN_TURN_M                {derived["r_min_turn"]:.4f}f',
        f'#define V_MIN_CRUISE_MPS            {derived["v_min_cruise"]:.4f}f',
        f'#define SLIP_GATE_SPEED_MPS         {d["slip_gate_speed_mps"]:.2f}f',
        f'#define SLIP_THRESH_SPIN_RAD_S      {d["slip_thresh_spin_rad_s"]:.2f}f',
        '',
        '// --- Digital Steering Servo PWM Configuration ---',
        f'#define SERVO_STEER_PWM_PIN         {s["pwm_pin"]}',
        f'#define SERVO_PWM_CHANNEL           {s["pwm_channel"]}',
        f'#define SERVO_PWM_FREQ_HZ           {s["pwm_freq_hz"]}',
        f'#define SERVO_PWM_RESOLUTION        {s["pwm_resolution"]}',
        f'#define SERVO_PULSE_MIN_US          {s["pulse_min_us"]:.1f}f',
        f'#define SERVO_PULSE_NEUTRAL_US      {s["pulse_neutral_us"]:.1f}f',
        f'#define SERVO_PULSE_MAX_US          {s["pulse_max_us"]:.1f}f',
        '',
        '// --- Control Loop Frequency & Timing ---',
        f'#define CONTROL_LOOP_FREQ_HZ        {t["control_loop_freq_hz"]}',
        f'#define CONTROL_LOOP_PERIOD_US      {derived["ctrl_period_us"]}',
        f'#define CONTROL_LOOP_DT_S           {derived["ctrl_dt_s"]:.5f}f',
        f'#define PI_UART_BAUD                {t["uart_baud"]}',
        f'#define TOF_UPDATE_HZ               {t["tof_hz"]:.1f}f',
        '',
        '// --- Costmap Geometry Bounds (Local Frame, +Y Fwd, +X Right) ---',
